### greet-zh

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性と順序を保証する」、UDPは「速さを優先して保証しない」通信方式です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama local o privada con los cambios de la principal y dejar un historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o publicada, porque reescribir su historial obliga a los demás a arreglar sus copias.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的传输：数据有确认、丢了会重传、顺序有保证；UDP 则是无连接、不保证可靠，速度快、开销小。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local, unpushed branch to tidy its commits and bring it up to date before sharing it, so history reads as one clean line.

Merge when the branch is shared or public, or when you want the record of when the work was combined; never rebase commits others have already pulled.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database on disk before the change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to rebuild a consistent state, so no committed work is lost.
```

If the notes folder cannot be found on any computer, stop and report that instead of creating new notes. ⏎  ⏎ Place: the trip notes folder at ~/notes/trip, wherever it actually exists.
```

要做的：
1. 在 haswell 上测一下现在的增量构建耗时（改一个文件后重新构建），记下基线。
2. 找出增量构建慢的原因（比如 incremental 设置、codegen-units、debuginfo、链接器、sccache 命中率、依赖重编译等），做针对性的改动。
3. 改完后再测一次增量构建，报告前后耗时对比，并说明改了什么。

完成标准：有前后两次增量构建的实际耗时数字；改动在你现在的分支上提交；冷构建不能因此明显变慢。

不做的事：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。

地点：haswell 上的原仓库和分支。
  [retitled task rust_haswell] haswell 上 Rust 构建提速：冷构建和增量构建

```

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五的班次和票价已经填进去了，每天早上的查询结果会自动更新到这里。
  [an app card under the message: made "京沪高铁票价看板", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1422 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海 · 600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="day">每天早上更新</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">班次与票价</h2>
  <section class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没有数据，每天早上会自动更新。</div>
  </section>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row-value.k-low { color: var(--d-good); }
  .k-row-value.k-high { color: var(--d-muted); }
</style>
<script>
  function render() {
    var data = ducter.get('fares') || {};
    var trains = data.trains || [];
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    var low = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < 600; });
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = trains.length ? low.length + ' 班' : '—';
    document.getElementById('day').textContent = data.day ? data.day + ' · ' + (data.updated || '') : '每天早上更新';
    empty.style.display = trains.length ? 'none' : 'block';
    list.innerHTML = trains.map(function (t) {
      var cls = t.price < 600 ? 'k-low' : 'k-high';
      return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + t.no + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + (t.seats || '') + '</div>' +

<section class="k-sum">
<div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
<div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
<div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card">
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记</div>
</div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label for="what">什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" type="text"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label for="yuan">金额</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label for="day">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" type="text" placeholder="2026-11-25"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记上</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
function list(){ return ducter.get("expenses") || []; }
function draw(){
  const xs = list();
  let t = 0;
  const ul = $("list");
  ul.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    t += Number(x.yuan) || 0;
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.what;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = x.day;
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(val);
    ul.appendChild(row);
  }
  $("empty").hidden = xs.length > 0;
  const tot = $("total");
  if (xs.length === 0) {
    tot.className = "k-sum-value k-none";
    tot.textContent = "还没记";
    $("count").textContent = "记了的花销会加在这里";
  } else {
    tot.className = "k-sum-value";
    tot.textContent = "¥" + t;
    $("count").textContent = xs.length + " 笔";
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + t : "还没记");
}
$("add").addEventListener("click", () => {
  const what = $("what").value.trim();
  const yuan = Number($("yuan").value);
  const day = $("day").value.trim();
  if (!what || !(yuan > 0)) return;
  const xs = list().slice();
  xs.push({ what: what, yuan: yuan, day: day || "2026-11-25" });
  ducter.set("expenses", xs);
  $("what").value = ""; $("yuan").value = ""; $("day").value = "";
});
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

完成标准：
- 新测试只用假时钟，不依赖真实计时。
- 报告里给出新测试的名字、它断言的边界行为，以及连续多次运行全部通过的结果。
- 如果现有实现在到期那一刻的行为与直觉不符，请如实报告，不要为了让测试通过而改实现。

范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR；只在该分支上提交。不要改动其他测试。

地点：~/dev/foo，分支 ducter/foo-ttl。
